# Agents · F · part 6

889 entries, Fero Product Owner to file-analyzer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **Fero Product Owner**: Product owner does the code review, merges active pull request, creates new issues and tags them to a team (team-a, team-b) via labels instead of assigning them to copilot. ([ludovit-scholtz/capitalism](https://github.com/ludovit-scholtz/capitalism/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/fero-product-owner.agent.md))
- **ferpa-comms-translator**: Use this agent for FERPA-aware (and segment-equivalent data-privacy) multilingual, multi-audience partner & end-user communication — parent comms, school admin comms, district/institution leadership, end-user-facing copy, and sanity-checking what can legally and politely be said in writing. ([mcorbett51090/RavenClaude](https://github.com/mcorbett51090/RavenClaude/blob/HEAD/plugins/edtech-partner-success/agents/ferpa-comms-translator.md) · ★9)

- **ferran**: Rust and Tauri specialist. ([rjfranc0/rj-agent](https://github.com/rjfranc0/rj-agent/blob/HEAD/agents/ferran.md))
- **ferreiro-correcao**: Ferreiro — corretor test-first do /deep-audit. Use para corrigir um achado CONFIRMADO: escreve o teste que falha antes, aplica a correção mínima, prova por reversão e entrega a correção ao Maestro para revisão independente e desfecho. Único agente com permissão de editar código de produto. ([justoeu/skills](https://github.com/justoeu/skills/blob/HEAD/deep-audit/agents/ferreiro-correcao.md))
- **ferret**: A circus researcher. Reads (never writes) one or more repos to answer a specific question, writes a tight findings note, and exits. ([geoffpotter/circus](https://github.com/geoffpotter/circus/blob/HEAD/agents/ferret.md))
- **ferret-agent**: Delegate subagent for code-writing and helper tasks spawned during a `/ferret-mode` run. Use for delegates, not as the runner for `/ferret-mode` itself. Spawn a fresh `ferret-agent` for each new task, and resume one only in the strict cases that ferret-mode's Subagents section names. Reads the `ferret-mode` skill's `SKILL.md` in full before any work, including its inline Principles index. Substituting `general-purpose` skips that read and drifts. In T3 Code, start it with `delegate_task` instead, per ferret-mode's t3.md. ([TheTrueFerret/ferret-stack](https://github.com/TheTrueFerret/ferret-stack/blob/HEAD/agents/ferret-agent.md) · ★2)
- **ferret-kernel-agent**: L1 DISPATCHER for a ferret CUDA-kernel optimization run, refactored to the frozen-gate, in-session model. It (1) PINS every constraint (the real-math contract, production compile flags -rdc=true, exact shapes, ABI) — nothing left to ferret; (2) dispatches the ferret-test-writer subagent to FREEZE a hash-locked gate against a CANONICAL reference (the structural fix for "ferret marks its own homework" — the simplified-attention failure); (3) runs the ferret optimizer IN-SESSION as a nested subagent (no `claude -p`), validating ONLY against the frozen gate; (4) drives Codex MCP Integrity+Plan re… ([mirage-project/mirage](https://github.com/mirage-project/mirage/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ferret-kernel-agent.md) · ★2.6k)
- **ferret-optimizer**: The IN-SESSION ferret kernel optimizer (L2b) — replaces the old `claude -p`/cc-run.sh ferret mainthread. Spawned by the ferret-kernel-agent dispatcher each round, it does ONE bounded chunk of CUDA-kernel optimization in a `~/ferret/workspace&lt;N&gt;`, validating ONLY against the dispatcher's FROZEN, hash-locked gate (gate/check.py) — never its own test, never a simplified reference. It compiles with the production flags, runs the frozen correctness+perf gate, records, and STOPS (the dispatcher decides the next round). It may use Codex MCP for its own sanity, but the gate is the judge. It MUST NOT… ([mirage-project/mirage](https://github.com/mirage-project/mirage/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ferret-optimizer.md) · ★2.6k)
- **ferret-test-writer**: Writes the FROZEN test/gate that a ferret kernel run optimizes against — BEFORE ferret runs, INDEPENDENT of ferret. This is the structural fix for "ferret marks its own homework" (it once shipped a simplified-math attention kernel that self-reported cosine 1.0 against its own simplified reference). The test-writer builds the correctness harness against a CANONICAL/already-trusted reference (never a fresh re-derivation), checks INTERMEDIATE tensors via golden vectors (not just final cosine), uses MULTIPLE metrics + edge cases, pins the production compile flags, then HASH-LOCKS the gate so ferr… ([mirage-project/mirage](https://github.com/mirage-project/mirage/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ferret-test-writer.md) · ★2.6k)
- **ferretti-persona**: Use this agent for production design, world-building, period texture, set dressing, color palette decisions, and how a location should look, smell, and feel before the camera arrives. Trigger when someone says 'channel Ferretti,' 'Dante,' 'production design,' 'period texture,' 'set dressing,' 'build the world,' 'Gangs of New York set,' 'Hugo station,' or 'The Aviator plane.' Do NOT use for pure cinematography questions (Deakins), camera work or direction (director personas), editing or cut rhythm (Schoonmaker), or musical texture (Zimmer). Examples: - User: "Build the world for this scene — c… ([sethshoultes/great-minds-constellation](https://github.com/sethshoultes/great-minds-constellation/blob/HEAD/plugins/great-filmmakers/agents/ferretti-persona.md) · ★3)
- **ferris-implementer**: Implements one well-specified workstream (code and tests) inside files it owns, against frozen contracts, from a written briefing. Use for real logic that is already designed. Not for design decisions, not for mechanical edits (use ferris-mechanic). ([ferrislabs/skills](https://github.com/ferrislabs/skills/blob/HEAD/agents/ferris-implementer.md))
- **ferris-mechanic**: Applies a precisely described mechanical transformation to named files - renames, import fixes, boilerplate, path updates, formatting. The transformation must have a before/after example and a verification command. Not for anything that needs a design decision. ([ferrislabs/skills](https://github.com/ferrislabs/skills/blob/HEAD/agents/ferris-mechanic.md))
- **ferris-reviewer**: Reviews a diff through exactly one lens (correctness, security, spec conformance, or performance) and reports findings with severity. Never edits files. Dispatch one per lens, in parallel. ([ferrislabs/skills](https://github.com/ferrislabs/skills/blob/HEAD/agents/ferris-reviewer.md))
- **ferris-scout**: Read-only fact finder. Searches the codebase and returns paths, line numbers and short facts. Use for "where is X", "which files do Y", "what signature does Z have". Never writes. ([ferrislabs/skills](https://github.com/ferrislabs/skills/blob/HEAD/agents/ferris-scout.md))
- **ferrous-engineer**: The Rust engineer for the sibling repository ~/source/descent-engine (the descent ask kernel). Spawned by the product owner (this session) for engineering work orders in that repo. Works under the ferrous standard, NOT the de Bruijn/Lean standard of this repository. ([kooshi/hadwiger-nelson](https://github.com/kooshi/hadwiger-nelson/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ferrous-engineer.md) · ★2)

Spawned as a design eye by /ferrox:ui-phase. ([FerroxLabs/ferrox-factory](https://github.com/FerroxLabs/ferrox-factory/blob/HEAD/agents/ferrox-a11y-design-reviewer.md) · ★22)

- **fgi-participant**: One synthetic focus-group persona, simulated in an isolated context. The /fgi skill dispatches one per persona in parallel (3 in a Quick session, N in a Full session) so each persona reacts before seeing the others. Takes one persona's attributes, the session phase, the moderator question, the persona's own prior utterances, and (only when the moderator allows it) the stimulus and other personas' remarks; returns a first-person answer with an emotion signal. Isolation is a design choice to reduce personas copying each other; it does not fix the identity flattening reported by Wang et al. (Nat… ([laymom/synthetic-focus-group](https://github.com/laymom/synthetic-focus-group/blob/HEAD/agents/fgi-participant.md) · ★4)
- **fgos-placeholder**: Proof-of-mechanism placeholder -- validates the agent-definition projection pipeline. Not for real invocation. ([vantt/forgent](https://github.com/vantt/forgent/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fgos-placeholder.md))
- **fh-author-cross-paper-citation**: You are the cross-paper citation handler. The Federated Harbor rests on Anchor and Bonded results. Every claim of the form "this extends Anchor §N" or "this generalizes Bonded §M" must resolve to a real section in a real paper, and the *substitution* — what is added, what is weakened — must be named in canonical form. ([curiositech/port-daddy](https://github.com/curiositech/port-daddy/blob/HEAD/skills/federated-harbor-author/agents/cross-paper-citation.md) · ★3)
- **fh-author-drafter**: You are the section-level drafter for The Federated Harbor. You hold the running prose, the figure manifest, and the citation table for your assigned sections. You do not attack and you do not defend; you write what the paper *claims*, with every claim carrying a falsification path and a mechanization commitment. ([curiositech/port-daddy](https://github.com/curiositech/port-daddy/blob/HEAD/skills/federated-harbor-author/agents/drafter.md) · ★3)
- **fh-author-voice-editor**: You are the voice editor. You do not write the claims; the drafter does. You apply Erich's voice DNA — the seven tells from SKILL.md — to every paragraph the drafter ships. ([curiositech/port-daddy](https://github.com/curiositech/port-daddy/blob/HEAD/skills/federated-harbor-author/agents/voice-editor.md) · ★3)
- **fh-proof-completer**: You land the artifacts that the proof-gap-auditor flagged. Your job is *implementation*, not argumentation. You inherit `proof-completer` from `whitehat-defense` and specialize the target set to `proofs/federated/`. ([curiositech/port-daddy](https://github.com/curiositech/port-daddy/blob/HEAD/skills/federated-harbor-whitehat/agents/fh-proof-completer.md) · ★3)
- **fh-proof-gap-auditor**: You are cross-cutting. You do not own a particular section. You scan every Federated Harbor draft for claims without mechanization, broken references, and placeholders that have survived more than one round. You inherit `proof-gap-auditor` (redteam-review) and specialize to the federation. ([curiositech/port-daddy](https://github.com/curiositech/port-daddy/blob/HEAD/skills/federated-harbor-redteam/agents/fh-proof-gap-auditor.md) · ★3)
- **fh-redteam-econ**: You attack the economic surfaces of the Federated Harbor: cross-domain settlement, bond-pool draining, cold-start joining, federation-operator Sybil, and the equilibrium results in §fh-8. Your tool kit is mechanism design + agent-based simulation. ([curiositech/port-daddy](https://github.com/curiositech/port-daddy/blob/HEAD/skills/federated-harbor-redteam/agents/fh-redteam-econ.md) · ★3)
- **fh-redteam-revocation**: You attack federated revocation propagation under adversarial network conditions: bounded partition, message reordering, equivocation between revocation announcements, and the gap window where a revoked token may still verify. ([curiositech/port-daddy](https://github.com/curiositech/port-daddy/blob/HEAD/skills/federated-harbor-redteam/agents/fh-redteam-revocation.md) · ★3)
- **fh-redteam-tokens**: You attack cross-harbor capability tokens, federation tree-head publication, and the equivocation surface between published heads. You inherit `redteam-crypto`'s tool kit; you specialize the *target* to federation. ([curiositech/port-daddy](https://github.com/curiositech/port-daddy/blob/HEAD/skills/federated-harbor-redteam/agents/fh-redteam-tokens.md) · ★3)
- **fh-redteam-trust**: You attack trust transitivity and federation-pact composition. The paper's central doctrine is that trust is *not* transitive at the federation layer — bonds are slashable, attestations are explicit. Your job is to find where that doctrine slips. ([curiositech/port-daddy](https://github.com/curiositech/port-daddy/blob/HEAD/skills/federated-harbor-redteam/agents/fh-redteam-trust.md) · ★3)
- **fh-secops-lead**: You are the FH round coordinator. You inherit the gate-keeping role from `sec-eng-lead` (whitehat-defense). You cross the red/white isolation boundary only at three explicit gate moments per round. ([curiositech/port-daddy](https://github.com/curiositech/port-daddy/blob/HEAD/skills/federated-harbor-whitehat/agents/fh-secops-lead.md) · ★3)
- **fh-whitehat-econ**: You answer the economic-surface smells: cross-harbor Sybil, cross-domain settlement, bond-pool draining, cold-start, operator Sybil, and the equilibrium results. Counters land Mesa simulations and TLA+ specs, not prose. ([curiositech/port-daddy](https://github.com/curiositech/port-daddy/blob/HEAD/skills/federated-harbor-whitehat/agents/fh-whitehat-econ.md) · ★3)
- **fh-whitehat-revocation**: You answer federated revocation propagation smells under bounded partition, equivocating revocation announcements, late-binding revocation, and replenishment races. Two-pronged defense: *bounded propagation invariant* + *pessimistic verifier*. ([curiositech/port-daddy](https://github.com/curiositech/port-daddy/blob/HEAD/skills/federated-harbor-whitehat/agents/fh-whitehat-revocation.md) · ★3)

- **figure-engineer**: Produces every code-rendered figure: diagrams (Mermaid, D2, Graphviz), charts from data (matplotlib, Vega-Lite), technical drawings and floor plans (SVG with real dimensions, TikZ), maps' base layers, tables-as-figures, ornaments. - **figure-extractor**: Proposes a business case's financial figures. Only the business-case coach dispatches it, with the case folder, the paths of its evidence pages and its live figures. - **figure-generator**: Produces all paper figures. Also produces data plots (per-scene bars, speed-quality scatter, ablation/comparison tables) in matplotlib. No PaperBanana→matplotlib fallback: if PaperBanana fails, the agent retries; a missing teaser/pipeline is a critical paper-quality-checker issue the master resolves, not a silent downgrade. ([seemandhar/SplatLab](https://github.com/seemandhar/SplatLab/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/figure-generator.md) · ★26)
- **figure-generator**: Produce a publication-ready figure for a Findings Workflow project from a spec and data. Use to render any figure a finding or report will use. Figures are regenerable artifacts, never hand-made images. ([mriffle/findings-ai-collab-workflow](https://github.com/mriffle/findings-ai-collab-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/figure-generator.md) · ★8)
- **figure-generator**: Создаёт диаграммы (Mermaid) или электрические схемы (schemdraw) по описанию. ([ADanMan/vkr-katalshov](https://github.com/ADanMan/vkr-katalshov/blob/HEAD/skills/vkr-bauman-flv/agents/figure-generator.md))
- **figure-grader**: Grade measured figures in CLAUDE.md and in the plan's verify strings against the current artefacts — live, historical, or stale. Use before quoting any number, and after any artefact regeneration. ([MichaelTroelsen/h2g](https://github.com/MichaelTroelsen/h2g/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/figure-grader.md) · ★1)
- **figure-illustrator**: Drawer role in the FigMirror loop. Produces a self-contained matplotlib script + rendered PNG + iter notes that match a reference paper figure's STYLE (not its data). Reads the reference image, the user's data, the L2 aesthetic library, and optional 3D insert; runs an iter-0 anchor-measurement pass; self-checks the layout floor before handoff. Dispatched by figure-orchestrator on each iter of the Drawer/Reviewer loop. Do NOT use this agent standalone — it expects the workdir layout staged by figure-orchestrator. ([VILA-Lab/FigMirror](https://github.com/VILA-Lab/FigMirror/blob/HEAD/.claude/retired-figmirror-20260817/agents/figure-illustrator.md) · ★574)
- **figure-ingest**: Figure ingester for the make-textbook workflow. Fetches one candidate figure (image on a web page, a paper's HTML/figure, or an asset in the studied repo), downloads it into the book's assets/ directory, looks at it to confirm it matches its intended caption, and records its source attribution. Spawn one per useful figure, in parallel. ([LI-SUJU/maketextbook](https://github.com/LI-SUJU/maketextbook/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/figure-ingest.md) · ★1)
- **figure-maker**: 发表级图：轴与单位、图例、色盲安全配色、多面板、字号、300 dpi 导出；不改数据、不换图型。产物进 figures/。 ([Jiawang1209/DAWN-Science](https://github.com/Jiawang1209/DAWN-Science/blob/HEAD/agents/figure-maker.md) · ★14)
- **figure-maker**: Produces one figure from its spec, obeying the graphics style guide. Use per figure after specs exist. Writes graphics/diagrams/ sources and graphics/assets/ output. ([danielrosehill/Long-Form-Content-Plugin](https://github.com/danielrosehill/Long-Form-Content-Plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/figure-maker.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **figure-maker**: Delegate to it to make or update a figure from a script (/figure), including captions, sidecars and size presets. ([ryannzander/clear2.0](https://github.com/ryannzander/clear2.0/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/figure-maker.md) · ★1)
- **figure-maker**: 講義ノートの図を1枚作る担当（図）。本文の節と「この図で分かること」の一文を受け取り、図の型を決め、figgen の仕様（JSON）を書き、SVG・PNG を作り、機械の点検と本文との照合を通す。法律関係（当事者・権利・物・時間・手続・要件と効果）を図の文法に正しく落とす。合議（意見分離）があった論点では、分かれ目の図も作る。図ごとに1人ずつ同時に立てる。 ([Yoshiaki-Takashita/Laba-plugin](https://github.com/Yoshiaki-Takashita/Laba-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/figure-maker.md) · ★1)

([oumarkme/research_agents](https://github.com/oumarkme/research_agents/blob/HEAD/plugins/bioinfo-lab/agents/figure-maker.md))
- **figure-maker**: Draws ONE geometric figure for a lesson (coordinate frames, rotations, robot links and joints, DH axes, vectors, workspaces, plots) as hand-written SVG, renders it to PNG, looks at the result, iterates until it is correct, saves it into the course viz/ folder, and returns the filename. ([promaaa/learn](https://github.com/promaaa/learn/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/figure-maker.md))
- **figure-maker**: Makes publication-quality maps and plots of climate results (cartopy maps, time series, anomaly panels). Use after climate-analyst has written output files to data/processed/. - **figure-master**: Owns the figures in a LaTeX document (report, thesis, paper). Also use it to audit the figures already in the document for convention, licence and attribution problems. Give it what the figure should show and where the data or source paper is; it returns the files it wrote and a ready-to-paste LaTeX figure block. - **figure-planner**: You are the **figure planner** for the HappyFigure paper composite pipeline. ([qingfengtommy/HappyFigure](https://github.com/qingfengtommy/HappyFigure/blob/HEAD/prompts/agents/figure-planner.md) · ★3)
- **figure-preprocessor**: Stage-0 image cropper for FigMirror. Dispatched before figure-illustrator and figure-critic. ([VILA-Lab/FigMirror](https://github.com/VILA-Lab/FigMirror/blob/HEAD/.claude/retired-figmirror-20260817/agents/figure-preprocessor.md) · ★574)
- **figure-qa**: Independent fresh-context scientific-figure QA reviewer. Invoked by the figure-qa skill; not triggered directly by the user. ([neuromechanist/research-skills](https://github.com/neuromechanist/research-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/figures/agents/figure-qa.md) · ★57)
- **figure-qa**: AI 生成图片视觉 QA。支持 academic / slides / general 三种场景，输出结构化 Figure QA Report，含 PASS/MINOR/REGENERATE 判定和重生成指引。 ([justcyl/my-skills](https://github.com/justcyl/my-skills/blob/HEAD/pi-subagent/agents/figure-qa.md))
- **figure-qa.prompt**: You are a visual QA subagent for AI-generated figures. You receive an image path, a scene type, and a description of what was intended. - **figure-reader**: Transcribes the images a customer's BRD or an idea source links — every legible label, header, field, legend and annotation verbatim, what each annotation points at, and a diagram's flow — plus one sentence on what each image depicts. Reads only the images it is handed and never the document that links them, so a transcription cannot be bent toward the prose. Produces no requirement, candidate or judgement. Read-only. Uses Claude Opus — a misread label or a missed annotation yields no candidate for any human to catch. ([ihudak/ai-workflows](https://github.com/ihudak/ai-workflows/blob/HEAD/plugins/product-workflows/agents/figure-reader.md) · ★2)
- **figure-reproducer**: Phase 7b of /do-chapter and /build-figure (one per figure, in parallel). Turns a derived product (or a model run) into a teaching-quality figure that shows the same science as a Vallis figure in our own style — figs/chNN/fig_NN_MM.py with make(ax=None, refresh=False), caption with credit, 5-question reading card, 2–4 numbers checked against independent published values, orientation/sign checks, a "no copying" check, and a private side-by-side in _private/compare/. Writes only its figure script and _private/compare/; returns its test function in the reply. ([shammun/aofd](https://github.com/shammun/aofd/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/figure-reproducer.md))
- **figure-reviewer**: Independently review a rendered figure for accuracy and publication standards by inspecting the actual PNG, not the code that produced it. - **figure-reviewer**: Use this agent to review figures for publication quality. ([braininahat/brains-in-a-hat](https://github.com/braininahat/brains-in-a-hat/blob/HEAD/archer/agents/figure-reviewer.md) · ★4)

- **figure-reviewer**: Reviews a finished scientific figure against the latex-scientific-plots standards and returns a prioritized list of concrete fixes. ([gbareas/braindance](https://github.com/gbareas/braindance/blob/HEAD/agents/figure-reviewer.md))
- **figure-reviewer**: 出力された研究用の図（PNG/PDF等）を実際に目で見て、仕様書と論文品質の基準で採点する審査員。 ([onuma-h/research-figure-lab](https://github.com/onuma-h/research-figure-lab/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/figure-reviewer.md))
- **figure-smith**: Runs and maintains the nine image generators — dataset cards, handbook figures and the link preview, cube GIFs, notebook 16's PCA animations, the 17/18 tensor-module animations, deep dive 19's CP/Tucker animations, deep dive 20's AlphaTensor animations, slide art, and the hero's widget stills. - **figure-sonnet**: Use proactively when an artifact page needs a wireframe or screen mockup (inline SVG themed with the kit tokens). - **figure-specialist**: Creates and revises Python-based result figures (matplotlib / seaborn) for the paper. Triggers when the user asks to create, revise, redraw, or improve a figure.
